[Geddon Arena - Center]
The hard-packed sand floor stretches out in all directions. The clanking of the iron gates that close behind the participants does not bode well. The center of the arena floor is covered with blood and broken pieces of armor and weapons. High above, in the stands, are cheering in anticipation of the next match.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Ad'in the Dragon,  Fynn the Zacotl,  Merau the Varanus,  Brand the Varanus,  Devin the Silvarin (laying on the ground),  Sammoth the Galirrim
In this room are: wooden torch, wooden torch, long staff
